# Ideas and Lean formalizations by taxonomy

Current follow-up for the **70 historically unresolved ideas**: [taxonomy-organized target search](unmatched-idea-followup.md). It records five narrower conditional fragments and distinguishes repair candidates, analogies, obstructions and unresolved claims. Historical statuses below are retained.

279 version-bound idea records across all 274 owner-designated unpublished PDF versions, with 226 pinned code links. All **233 formerly screened versions** now have substantive decisions from selected complete pages beyond screening; the earlier 41 comparisons remain intact. No full-manuscript or independent kernel audit is claimed.

These are assistant-proposed interpretations, not statements of what R.A. Jacob Martone understood or when. Owner statements and understanding dates remain null. Mathematical correspondence does not establish influence, priority or attribution. Attribution clarification remains tracked in [issue #254](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/issues/254).

A **matched-subclaim** covers only the named finite lemma or data contract. A **conditional-correspondence** needs the explicit source instantiation and remaining theorems. An **analogy-only** link asserts no theorem equivalence. An **unresolved** idea has no established Lean target. Source inspection is not compilation or an independent kernel audit. Source titles claiming proofs remain bibliographic titles.

Expanded code baseline: `549d145e5ab7b9faaacab46fd9b6123d907ee1f3`. The original four links retain their earlier pinned commit and evidence unchanged. Exact PDF SHA-256 identities distinguish every version; page numbers below are physical PDF pages. Hypothesis lists are passage-level transcriptions or scoped summaries, not complete formal restatements.

- [Algebra (21 ideas)](#taxonomy-algebra)

## Algebra

### Conjectures and Proofs for Galois-Theoretic Extensions of Automorphic L-Functions — `1574517a994e`

**unresolved** · unlabeled version · `idea:unpublished-v3:1574517a994e89cc:motivic-period-orbits`

Later target-search review: **unresolved**, main claim unresolved. [Exact follow-up evidence](unmatched-idea-followup.md#1574517a994e89cc-motivic-period-orbits).

Paper: [PDF page 1](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/1574517a994e--Proof_Draft_for_Zeros_on_the_Critical_Line__Riemann_Zeta_Function_and_Dirichlet_L_functions (4).pdf#page=1>), [PDF page 2](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/1574517a994e--Proof_Draft_for_Zeros_on_the_Critical_Line__Riemann_Zeta_Function_and_Dirichlet_L_functions (4).pdf#page=2>) — Conjectures 1–3 and proof sketches.

Exact PDF SHA-256: `1574517a994e89cc658f59f7f3bafff2eacaa601ef6398e411aa8f3d86c3ff09`.

Read physical pages: 1, 2. Exact cited text anchors: p1: “Conjecture 1:” (page-text SHA-256 `0c52294d74ab6084de944ef31a6358ebfa5a3c995a711530b3669360c8f68049`); p2: “Proof of Conjecture 3:” (page-text SHA-256 `54860375a64a792f74356ff7e66e13e03beb88ab7e58530071d737b1986baf5f`).

Paper idea (assistant reading): The manuscript proposes a motivic-Galois action on noncritical values, independence of conjugates and a field containing infinitely many algebraically independent periods.

Proposed interpretation: No exact motivic-period action or transcendence theorem was established in the maintained finite-Galois/descent modules; those modules cannot replace these proposed objects.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- A specified category of motives, automorphic realization and period comparison would be needed; algebraic independence is explicitly assumed in the second conjecture.

Code: **no matching declaration established in this pass**.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- Distinguish noncritical in the motivic critical-value sense from Re(s)≠1/2; define the exact values and realizations.
- Specify the acting group and period torsor and prove orbit-generation claims. Baker’s theorem does not establish arbitrary motivic period independence.
- A group orbit or transcendental generator does not by itself produce infinitely many algebraically independent periods; state and prove the required transcendence-degree assertion.

### High-Dimensional Positivity Constraints via Algebraic K-Theory and Derived Categories — `fa1ed0e14550`

**analogy-only** · unlabeled version · `idea:unpublished-v3:fa1ed0e14550900e:kzero-versus-positive-euler-form`

Paper: [PDF page 1](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/fa1ed0e14550--A (12).pdf#page=1>), [PDF page 2](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/fa1ed0e14550--A (12).pdf#page=2>) — §2.1–2.2 and §3 derived positivity.

Exact PDF SHA-256: `fa1ed0e14550900e8ea72a3e23e286a6b5a52d347e8acd33aa1064bc078b9c12`.

Read physical pages: 1, 2. Exact cited text anchors: p1: “Euler Form and Positivity” (page-text SHA-256 `19bead9990d181f32c54878f070ce806eeee3b949a0183d296cf803e607cd56f`); p2: “Localization in K-Theory” (page-text SHA-256 `5185274690552af8c820195c67e25cef5f42dbc5b08975a6b0d2f41aef71f7ad`).

Paper idea (assistant reading): K₀, an alternating Ext Euler pairing, a localization sequence and derived-category localization are used to claim positive boundary pairings and off-line residue suppression.

Proposed interpretation: The Lean group-completion map is only a K₀-shaped functorial algebraic fragment; it does not construct geometric K₀, localization exactness or an Euler positivity theorem.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- A precise geometric category and finite Ext/Euler data, with properness/smoothness and support hypotheses appropriate to the claimed operations.

Code: [`GRWTSK.Algebra.KTheory.GrothendieckKZero.map_comp`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/549d145e5ab7b9faaacab46fd9b6123d907ee1f3/GRWTSK/Algebra/KTheory/GrothendieckPort.lean#L33-L39), lines 33–39; Git blob `492815935a542ad989c1109225d52ee0170f3ee8`.

Maps of supplied commutative stable-object monoids induce functorial maps of universal group completions. This K₀-shaped algebra does not construct geometric K₀, Ext positivity or a residue realization. Pinned source inspected; no new compilation or independent kernel audit performed.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- Identify the stable-object monoid and relations with the actual geometric K₀. Coherent-sheaf exact-sequence relations require a genuine category comparison.
- The alternating Euler form is not generally strictly positive; prove the specific sheaf/degree hypotheses and the correct pairing formula, including dualization.
- Define the residue consumer and prove the comparison from cohomology to analytic zeros. A named localization functor cannot replace that bridge.

- Specify and justify the affine/quantum variants instead of adding unnamed correction terms.

For any additive-group sequence, the finite signed sum of adjacent residuals equals the endpoint difference. No decay or arithmetic cancellation bound follows. Pinned source inspected; no new compilation or independent kernel audit performed.

Code: [`GRWTSK.Analysis.ErrorBounds.ContractingResidualBudget.variable_budget_vanishes`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/549d145e5ab7b9faaacab46fd9b6123d907ee1f3/GRWTSK/Analysis/ErrorBounds/ContractingResidualBudget.lean#L80-L110), lines 80–110; Git blob `753f3162d343c67447cd2f8681291821b50b785d`.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- An O(1) increment does not imply bounded partial sums or convergence to zero; even increments tending to zero do not imply that conclusion.
- O(√p log p) does not become smaller than log p, and the proposed correction doubles rather than automatically removes the printed log contribution.
- Preserve the explicit RH/GRH premises of §11; a conditional stabilization lemma cannot prove those same premises.

Paper idea (assistant reading): The unified propagation theorem sums O(x^β) domain metrics and cycles, asserting unbounded growth contradicts a logarithmic bound stated under RH.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- Actual independent domain transfers and an unconditional bound on the unchanged arithmetic source.

Code: [`GRWTSK.Analysis.Waveform.polynomial_return`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/549d145e5ab7b9faaacab46fd9b6123d907ee1f3/GRWTSK/Analysis/Asymptotics/PolynomialErrorBound.lean#L17-L32), lines 17–32; Git blob `5833a6cc857304794e72a46f8efdb6327e81e96a`.

Given X=R+e on T≥0, nonnegative constants and separate real-power bounds for R and |e|, X is bounded by the summed constant at the maximum exponent. This is an upper bound, not divergence or uniform boundedness. Pinned source inspected; no new compilation or independent kernel audit performed.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- O(x^β) or O(Nx^β) is not a lower bound or a proof of divergence; constants and cycle dependence must be controlled.
- The comparator O(log²x) is explicitly asserted under RH, so using it to exclude off-line zeros is circular unless proved independently for the exact source.
- Prove arithmetic-to-motivic/modular/geometric transfers. Function-field purity and random-matrix predictions do not automatically constrain a number-field zero.

Read physical pages: 2, 3. Exact cited text anchors: p2: “Substitute f (u) into the Mellin transform:” (page-text SHA-256 `5813992400e5aaeab39c636a389edfa724725d9b1cb66d17d3bf294e002bf69c`); p3: “Lemma 2.1 (Harmonic duality).” (page-text SHA-256 `74be1483c4ee53d23858f8021c4205d8b89c91329790933419c34efa112b4d58`).

Paper idea (assistant reading): A damped coefficient Mellin integral is used to assert meromorphic continuation of arbitrary Dirichlet series, followed by a claimed reflection law for a critical-line Laplace transform.

Proposed interpretation: The Lean tail-integrability criterion is a possible conditional component for a properly bounded transform. It does not construct analytic continuation or the claimed reflection symmetry.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- Only absolute convergence for Re(s)>1 is initially assumed for the Dirichlet series.
- The harmonic transform is initially defined for Re(s)>0; no majorant or functional equation is proved for this general class.

Code: [`EulerP04RH.right_tail_bound`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/549d145e5ab7b9faaacab46fd9b6123d907ee1f3/GRWTSK/Analysis/Asymptotics/SignedEnergyDecomposition.lean#L87-L99), lines 87–99; Git blob `09c7901370857dc6f95e1f3e81fa7416ab56c1cb`.

Given m>1, H>0, AEStrongMeasurable F on (H,∞) and the pointwise norm bound A*t^(−m), the tail is integrable and its integral norm is at most A*H^(1−m)/(m−1). No Mellin continuation is constructed. Pinned source inspected; no new compilation or independent kernel audit performed.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- The integral of e^(−nu)u^(s−1) is n^(−s)Γ(s), not Γ(s). Specify the scaled identity and justify sum/integral interchange.
- Meromorphy of Γ does not continue an otherwise uncontinued Mellin transform. Supply the small-u expansion/subtractions and a genuine continuation theorem for the chosen coefficient class.
- The displayed exponential substitution in HL(1−s) is not an equality to HL(s). Derive the exact transformed functional equation instead of dropping a factor.
- For a Lean tail instance supply measurability, a valid majorant on Ioi H and m>1; these control a tail, not the missing small-u continuation.

Paper idea (assistant reading): The argument combines a high-probability square-root estimate, an ergodic mean-zero assertion and Fourier phase cancellation to claim bounded cumulative error.

Proposed interpretation: Lean distinguishes an upper-sublinear nonnegative budget from its little-o form. That comparison formalizes the weaker scale of a vanishing normalized sum, not a bounded unnormalized sum.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- An actual probability space, bounded centered variables with the required dependence control, or a specified measure-preserving ergodic system.

Code: [`GRWTSK.Analysis.SublinearDyadic.upperSublinear_iff_isLittleO`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/549d145e5ab7b9faaacab46fd9b6123d907ee1f3/GRWTSK/Analysis/Asymptotics/SublinearDyadic.lean#L29-L45), lines 29–45; Git blob `91db37fec2983da6de753be5f3e8e6cad1295d75`.

For a nonnegative sequence, the epsilon upper-sublinear definition is equivalent to f=o(n). No boundedness, logarithmic rate or centering theorem follows. Pinned source inspected; no new compilation or independent kernel audit performed.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- Prove centering and an arithmetic probabilistic/ergodic model; ergodic convergence goes to the mean and gives no automatic rate.
- E_N/N→0 allows unbounded E_N. Distinguish almost-sure, high-probability, deterministic and uniform conclusions.
- Equation (7) replaces a sum of two oscillatory sequences by their product-frequency expansion. Supply the actual signed identity; additive cross-domain error is not their product.
- For Fourier bounds prove nonresonance of the relevant discrete phases modulo 2π and summable coefficient/small-divisor control.

### Formalized Error Bound for Mixed Automorphic Forms — `bbd0d7b6e47a`

**conditional-correspondence** · unlabeled version · `idea:unpublished-v3:bbd0d7b6e47a4956:mixed-counting-budget`

Paper: [PDF page 1](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/bbd0d7b6e47a--Formalized_Error_Bound_for_Mixed_Automorphic_Forms (1).pdf#page=1>), [PDF page 2](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/bbd0d7b6e47a--Formalized_Error_Bound_for_Mixed_Automorphic_Forms (1).pdf#page=2>) — equations (3)–(7), decomposition and reported cumulative bounds.

Exact PDF SHA-256: `bbd0d7b6e47a4956d604a42ca58c85c6512c84ea4e332f6961dde6fac118bbf0`.

Read physical pages: 1, 2. Exact cited text anchors: p1: “Error Term Decomposition” (page-text SHA-256 `a9b4a3dcf541254fe8a2da7a20ee7590bbef7df4db78f4e7de75d6f141b99680`); p2: “Numerical Verification” (page-text SHA-256 `317b02b97a83002909c814b0090cd15fb0748e459007526fa5f20b5569f74a5d`).

Paper idea (assistant reading): The Rankin–Selberg counting-error model combines deterministic oscillations, a stochastic variance and a proposed Kalman correction, then states square-root and square-root/logarithmic cumulative bounds.

Proposed interpretation: The polynomial-return theorem is a possible exact assembly step once a literal main/residual decomposition and proved bounds are supplied. It is not a Kalman-filter or Rankin–Selberg theorem.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- A precisely defined counting function and main term; actual model parameters, probability law, correction operator and N>1.

Code: [`GRWTSK.Analysis.Waveform.polynomial_return`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/549d145e5ab7b9faaacab46fd9b6123d907ee1f3/GRWTSK/Analysis/Asymptotics/PolynomialErrorBound.lean#L17-L32), lines 17–32; Git blob `5833a6cc857304794e72a46f8efdb6327e81e96a`.

Given X=R+e on T≥0, nonnegative constants and separate real-power bounds for R and |e|, X is bounded by the summed constant at the maximum exponent. This is an upper bound, not divergence or uniform boundedness. Pinned source inspected; no new compilation or independent kernel audit performed.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- Derive the asserted stochastic law and filter from the actual automorphic counting source, and prove the correction identity rather than prescribing φ_n.
- The O(√N) contribution in (6) does not imply the stronger O(√N/log N) claim in (7). Prove the additional cancellation or weaken the latter claim.
- Record signed versus absolute error, probability qualifier and constants. The denominator log N excludes N=1 and requires a separate finite-prefix treatment.

### Fredholm–Hodge Comparator Naturality and Endpoint Reversal — `82f838ae5214`

**conditional-correspondence** · Revision 1.1 · `idea:unpublished-v2:82f838ae5214a4e5`

Paper: [PDF page 8](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/82f838ae5214--P10.pdf#page=8>) — Theorem 6.1 (P10-T03).

Exact PDF SHA-256: `82f838ae5214a4e5699c952b7b5871ffb0137a5a2deb5986d67bfd61cc2c9f93`.

Paper idea (assistant reading): Comparator normalization must use an independently nowhere-zero gauge and preserve the same endpoint data.

Proposed interpretation: Lean has actual holomorphic units and gauge transport of sections on a fixed cover. This is a related geometric interface, not an identification of the paper’s Fredholm and Hodge endpoints.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- Both endpoint expressions and normalizers have the stated common domain; the gauge is independently holomorphic and nowhere zero.

Code: [`GRWTSK.Analysis.Complex.HolomorphicLineGauge.Gauge.familyEquiv`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/fa27fde9b4df2aedd203679e832c0956c3e12889/GRWTSK/Analysis/Complex/HolomorphicLineGauge.lean#L137-L153), lines 137–153; Git blob `02bce81ab498f01b07b51b41e21eed45bcc2e742`.

Supplied holomorphic unit gauges transport twisted families on the same indexed cover. Pinned source inspected; no compilation or independent kernel audit performed in this mapping pass.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- Construct endpoint sections, their common domains and normalizers as actual holomorphic units.
- Prove the quotient and dense-core endpoint identities and align the same cocycles and source.
- Gauge transport does not prove endpoint reversal, equality of zero loci or RH.

- Derive a complete residual/error bound with strict contraction or a valid local Newton/Kantorovich hypothesis and a controlled forcing term. A small update is not a small distance to a root without conditioning.
- J+λI is a shifted inverse; distinguish it from the normal-equations Tikhonov inverse and prove the actual bias/error of whichever method is used.
- Retain the reported infinite-error cases and do not promote the proposed damping or CRT-inspired coverage analogy to global convergence.

### Prime-Complete Logarithmic Frame Faithfulness — `3e667d873ecf`

**analogy-only** · Revision 1.2 · `idea:unpublished-v2:3e667d873ecf5260`

Paper: [PDF page 8](</Users/think/Desktop/MathematicsLibrary/NumberTheory/3e667d873ecf--P09 (2).pdf#page=8>) — Theorems 4.1–4.3 (P09-T01–T03).

Exact PDF SHA-256: `3e667d873ecf526057217b7a0737b0d4aa4f0be3945e7b7bf333c998c3fe1c32`.

Paper idea (assistant reading): Bessel upper control and positive Gram form do not by themselves ensure a faithful observation on the reconstruction image; a restricted lower frame floor is needed.

Proposed interpretation: The Lean empty-family counterexample makes the upper-bound/lower-reconstruction distinction explicit. It is a scope guard, not the manuscript’s frame equivalence.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- A finite-dimensional nonzero represented source, an injective reconstruction J with image M, and an independently defined Bessel observation W.
- The claimed equivalences concern W restricted to M; a lower frame bound is not supplied by the Bessel upper bound.

Code: [`EulerP04RH.ResearchObstructions.upper_bessel_does_not_imply_reconstruction`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/fa27fde9b4df2aedd203679e832c0956c3e12889/GRWTSK/Analysis/InnerProductSpace/LogarithmicCharacterGramBounds.lean#L253-L261), lines 253–261; Git blob `db2cb85bcb679383a4a4b7664fb6fbe644ed2422`.

An empty-family counterexample separates upper Bessel control from lower reconstruction. Pinned source inspected; no compilation or independent kernel audit performed in this mapping pass.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- Identify the actual P07 image and independently defined prime observation.
- Prove restricted injectivity and a positive lower frame bound, then the finite-prefix/dimension and decoder statements in the correct spaces.
- The finite logarithmic orbit is not Haar orthogonality and no all-window prime completeness theorem follows from this guard.

- A threshold of order √N log N yields that threshold, not O(log N). Bounded variance and weak dependence alone do not supply the displayed Hoeffding assumptions.
- Almost-sure normalized convergence does not imply O(log N), bounded sums or uniform deterministic control. Prove the needed rate and arithmetic realization.
- Retain the deterministic main/secondary terms and all resonance/small-divisor costs in the claimed mixed-source error.

- Retain the deterministic main/secondary terms and all resonance/small-divisor costs in the claimed mixed-source error.

- Reflection allows symmetric off-line pairs. A separate zero-exclusion theorem is needed before concluding Re(ρ)=1/2.

Proposed interpretation: With K=(1+T)^q, ASBNP04.amortized_iteration is the displayed finite induction step. No nonnegativity of the intermediate potential is required.

Paper hypotheses and scope:

- At fixed T≥0, masses ω_j≥0 and common C,q≥0; set K=(1+T)^q≥0.
- The potential obeys φ_(j+1)≤exp(Cω_j)φ_j+Cω_j K. The finite induction does not require intermediate φ_j≥0.

Code: [`ASBNP04.amortized_iteration`](https://github.com/grwtsk/math/blob/fa27fde9b4df2aedd203679e832c0956c3e12889/GRWTSK/Analysis/Asymptotics/MassWeightedRecurrence.lean#L28-L72), lines 28–72; Git blob `fa1e14a598dd9616dc10c43e51ffa3a5d803c2c5`.

Missing bridges and uncertainty:

- The code assumes the recurrence; it does not construct the actual source-derived reserve or an admissible full-prime chain.
- The harmonic-mass estimate, polynomial initial bound, terminal reserve nonnegativity and transfer to P04 must be supplied to close the whole paper theorem.
- The paper’s universal-interface obstruction is not removed by this conditional finite lemma.
